Compute pairwise Euclidean distances\n    dist_matrix = cdist(centroids, expert_points, metric='euclidean')\n\n    matches = []\n    used_centroids = set()\n    used_experts = set()\n\n    # Get all distances with indices, sorted by distance\n    pairs = []\n    for i in range(len(centroids)):\n        for j in range(len(expert_points)):\n            pairs.append((dist_matrix[i, j], i, j))\n\n    pairs.sort(key=lambda x: x[0])\n\n    for dist, c_idx, e_idx in pairs:\n        if dist > max_dist:\n            break\n        if c_idx not in used_centroids and e_idx not in used_experts:\n            matches.append((c_idx, e_idx, dist))\n            used_centroids.add(c_idx)\n            used_experts.add(e_idx)\n\n    return matches\n\n\ndef compute_f1_delta_for_image(citsci_points, expert_points, min_samples, epsilon, shape_weight):\n    \"\"\"\n    Run DBSCAN on citizen science points for a single image and compute F1 and delta.\n    \"\"\"\n    n_expert = len(expert_points)\n\n    # Get unique clusters (excluding noise label -1)\n    unique_labels = set(labels)\n    unique_labels.discard(-1)\n\n    if len(unique_labels) == 0:\n        # No clusters found\n        return 0.0, np.nan\n\n    # Compute delta (average Euclidean distance of matched pairs)\n    delta = np.mean([m[2] for m in matches])\n\n    return f1, delta\n\n\ndef evaluate_hyperparameters(min_samples, epsilon, shape_weight, citsci_by_image, expert_by_image, all_images):\n    \"\"\"\n    Evaluate a single hyperparameter combination across all images.\n    \"\"\"\n    f1_scores = []\n    deltas = []\n\n    for img in all_images:\n        citsci_pts = citsci_by_image.get(img, np.array([]))\n        expert_pts = expert_by_image.get(img, np.array([]))\n\n        if len(expert_pts) == 0:\n            # Skip images with no expert annotations\n            continue\n\n        f1, delta = compute_f1_delta_for_image(\n            citsci_pts, expert_pts, min_samples, epsilon, shape_weight\n        )\n\n        f1_scores.append(f1)\n        if not np.isnan(delta):\n            deltas.append(delta)\n\n    if len(f1_scores) == 0:\n        return None\n\n    avg_f1 = np.mean(f1_scores)\n    avg_delta = np.mean(deltas) if len(deltas) > 0 else np.nan\n\n    return {\n        'F1': avg_f1,\n        'delta': avg_delta,\n        'min_samples': min_samples,\n        'epsilon': epsilon,\n        'shape_weight': shape_weight\n    }\n\n\ndef find_pareto_frontier(results):\n    \"\"\"\n    Find Pareto-optimal points.\n    Maximize F1, minimize delta.\n    A point is Pareto-optimal if no other point has higher F1 AND lower delta.\n    \"\"\"\n    pareto = []\n\n    for i, r1 in enumerate(results):\n        is_dominated = False\n        for j, r2 in enumerate(results):\n            if i == j:\n                continue\n            # r2 dominates r1 if r2 has higher or equal F1 AND lower or equal delta\n            # with at least one strict inequality\n            if (r2['F1'] >= r1['F1'] and r2['delta'] <= r1['delta'] and\n                (r2['F1'] > r1['F1'] or r2['delta'] < r1['delta'])):\n                is_dominated = True\n                break\n\n        if not is_dominated:\n            pareto.append(r1)\n\n    return pareto\n\n\ndef main():\n    print(\"Loading data...\")\n    citsci_df = pd.read_csv('/root/data/citsci_train.csv')\n    expert_df = pd.read_csv('/root/data/expert_train.csv')\n\n    # Group data by file_rad\n    print(\"Grouping data by image...\")\n    citsci_by_image = {}\n    for img, group in citsci_df.groupby('file_rad'):\n        citsci_by_image[img] = group[['x', 'y']].values\n\n    expert_by_image = {}\n    for img, group in expert_df.groupby('file_rad'):\n        expert_by_image[img] = group[['x', 'y']].values\n\n    # Run grid search in parallel\n    print(\"Running grid search...\")\n    results = Parallel(n_jobs=-1, verbose=10)(\n        delayed(evaluate_hyperparameters)(\n            ms, eps, sw, citsci_by_image, expert_by_image, all_images\n        )\n        for ms, eps, sw in param_combinations\n    )\n\n    # Filter out None results and those with F1 <= 0.5 or NaN delta\n    valid_results = [\n        r for r in results\n        if r is not None and r['F1'] > 0.5 and not np.isnan(r['delta'])\n    ]\n    print(f\"Valid results (F1 > 0.5): {len(valid_results)}\")\n\n    if len(valid_results) == 0:\n        print(\"No valid results found!\")\n        return\n\n    #
